# OMADA_TOOLS.md — Complete Tool Catalogue

> **Based on:** Omada OpenAPI Spec v0.1  
> **Controller version coverage:** not specified in spec  
> **Generated:** 2026-03-17  
> **Total operations:** 1648 (across all spec files, 01-account through 17-abnormal-detect)

This file is the authoritative index of all MCP tools derivable from the Omada OpenAPI spec.
One row per HTTP operation. Tools with both GET and POST have two rows.

**Notes:**
- `✅ Implemented` — a matching tool file exists in `src/tools/`
- `⬜ Not implemented` — operation exists in spec but no tool file found
- Rows marked `(not found in spec files)` are implemented tools whose API paths do not appear in the current spec documents but are present in `src/omadaClient/`
